## NB39 — unpark with isolation

**The ISOLATION LAW — recorded (CLAUDE.md §27).** The §24 freeze is replaced by isolation, so work unparks without endangering the money chain: **(a)** the Price Engine chain's files, launch path, and process list are off-limits to everything else; **(b)** every other engine is its own **diary-only** process (no orders) until it passes its own audit + smoke check; **(c)** others start only after the 08:15 READY line and only if measured free RAM ≥ the reopen floor + that process's own measured footprint, and are **killed first** under memory pressure (the chain never); **(d)** their failures are **quiet** (✅/⏱ + a diary/held log, never 🙋); **(e)** site work is front-end only. Root swap stays owner-gated.

**UI-1 defect 5 — done (front-end, code-verified).** `v8_site` now **strips the Deliverables tab from the v2 nav** (UI-1 item 5, a later OWNER-SAID, reverses R-V2-PAGE-OWNERS item 1 — later item-specific instruction governs; this also corrected the NB32 test I'd aligned the other way). `test_v8_site` green. It's a site builder — front-end, off the money-chain path (§27e) — so master's LIVE code (`32df791c4`) is untouched; the site loop applies the strip on its next rebuild.

Doing seven UI fixes + a whole new site rushed at ~2:52 am, unscreenshot-verified, racing the loop, is exactly the NB8 unverified-UI mistake the original plan avoids by scheduling 15:45. The **8 engine items** (diary-only, §27a–d) and the **twin-run clone execution** are the after-Thursday-close batch.

105	def _short_reason(sentence):
106	    """A short plain phrase from a real signal sentence, e.g. 'when VWAP is in its lowest 20%'."""
107	    body = sentence.split(":", 1)[-1] if ":" in sentence else sentence
108	    body = body.split("→")[0].split("->")[0]         # cut at the arrow
109	    body = body.replace("daily,", "").replace("daily ", "").strip(" ,.")
110	    return (body[:60] + "…") if len(body) > 62 else body

364	    rows). Real columns bind — name, volume (rvol), trend — and the readings the screener does not carry

367	    stock is 'ready' when its screener row is not stale. The design's 30 procedurally-generated stocks are
368	    dropped."""
369	    import json
370	    def _load(path, key=None):

384	        out.append({
385	            "name": sym, "range": DASH, "rc": GREY,
386	            "mom": DASH, "calm": DASH, "flow": DASH,
387	            "vol": ("%.1fx" % rvol) if isinstance(rvol, (int, float)) else DASH,
388	            "gap": DASH,
389	            "met": "signal" if sig else DASH, "mc": GREEN if sig else GREY,
390	            "m5": DASH, "m20": DASH, "gapd": DASH,
391	            "trend": r.get("trend") or DASH, "r60": DASH,
392	            "sig": sig, "ready": not r.get("stale", False),
393	        })
394	    out.sort(key=lambda x: (not x["sig"], x["name"]))     # real signals first, then alphabetical
395	    ready_n = sum(1 for x in out if x["ready"])
396	    on, off, onFg, offFg = "#5DCAA5", "#161920", "#0f1115", "#b8b6ae"
397	    return {
398	        "rows": out, "count": len(out), "note": "signals first, then the rest of the watch list",
399	        "allBg": on, "allFg": onFg, "sigBg": off, "sigFg": offFg, "readyBg": off, "readyFg": offFg,
400	        "pickAll": "", "pickSig": "", "pickReady": "", "typeQuery": "",
401	        "_ready_n": ready_n, "_total": len(out),   # consumed by _fix_stocks, stripped before render
402	    }

tool_result: 195	def _data_data():
196	    """The Data page: one row per recorded stream, from the real stores (vitals._streams). Every field is a
197	    real value or empty — no sample number. The per-lane detail stays closed (open=False), so the sc-if detail
198	    fields are not required."""
199	    from scripts import vitals
200	    used_by = {"Order-book depth": "E1 (the moment light)", "Option chains": "E4 (options)",
201	               "NSE/BSE two-exchange": "E12 (two-exchange)", "Retail positioning": "E14 (positioning)"}
202	    JUDGE_DAYS = 20                       # a stream can be judged once it has ~20 recorded sessions (declared)
203	    lanes = []
204	    for name, s in (vitals._streams() or {}).items():
205	        rows = s.get("rows_10min")
206	        recording = bool(rows) or bool(s.get("last"))
207	        lanes.append({
208	            "name": name,
209	            "status": "Recording data" if recording else "No data today",
210	            "statusColor": "#5DCAA5" if recording else "#F09595",
211	            "rows": rows if rows is not None else "—",
212	            "today": rows if rows is not None else "—",
213	            "last": s.get("last") or "—",
214	            "days": 0, "countdown": JUDGE_DAYS,          # days recorded / days until it can be judged
215	            "strip": "", "stripColor": "#23262d", "sample": "", "usedBy": used_by.get(name, "the desk"),
216	            "open": False, "toggle": "",
217	        })
218	    return {"lanes": lanes}

def build(write=True):
    page = ("<!doctype html><html lang='en'><head><meta charset='utf-8'>"
            "<meta name='viewport' content='width=device-width,initial-scale=1'><title>Deliverables</title>"
            "<style>" + CSS + "</head><body><main><h1>Deliverables</h1>"

    if problems:
        raise ValueError("deliverables page contains banned words: %s" % problems[:6])
    if write:
        os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "index.html"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(page)
    return page


def _scrub(page):
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", page)                 # visible text only
    hits = []
    for pat in BANNED:
        for m in re.findall(pat, text):
            hits.append(m)
    return sorted(set(hits))

29	def _account():
30	    try:
31	        from engine1_rebuild import paper_account as ACC, gate
32	        a = ACC.account("E1")
33	        held = gate.held()
34	        line = "Paper trades: %s" % ("OFF — held for a fix" if held else "ON")

36	            html.escape(str(o.get("stock", ""))), html.escape(str(o.get("direction", ""))),
37	            html.escape(str(o.get("entry", ""))), ACC.rupees(o.get("uPnL") or 0)) for o in a["open_positions"])
38	        return a, line, opens

40	        return {"equity": 200000.0, "open_positions": [], "open_count": 0}, "Paper trades: unknown", ""

43	def _pattern_cards():
44	    """Score cards from the 937-call book, grouped by FAMILY (the ruler). Per family: cases, win rate,
45	    confidence-adjusted win rate (Wilson LB across its cases), average move after costs (mean net R), and the
46	    stage. Every number computed from the blotter + the certified registry — nothing typed."""
47	    fams = {}
48	    if os.path.exists(BLOTTER):
49	        for r in csv.DictReader(open(BLOTTER, encoding="utf-8")):
50	            f = str(r.get("family", "")).split("+")[0]
51	            d = fams.setdefault(f, {"cases": 0, "wins": 0, "sumR": 0.0})
52	            d["cases"] += 1
53	            try:
54	                rr = float(r.get("result_R") or 0)
55	            except Exception:
56	                rr = 0.0
57	            d["sumR"] += rr
58	            d["wins"] += 1 if str(r.get("correct")).strip().lower() == "true" else 0
59	    rows = []
60	    for f, d in sorted(fams.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1]["cases"]):
61	        n, w = d["cases"], d["wins"]
62	        wr = w / n if n else 0
63	        lb = _wilson_lb(w, n)
64	        avg = d["sumR"] / n if n else 0
65	        rows.append((_RULER.get(f, f), n, wr, lb, avg))
66	    return rows
67	
68	
69	def _wilson_lb(w, n, z=1.96):
70	    if n == 0:
71	        return 0.0
72	    p = w / n
73	    denom = 1 + z * z / n
74	    centre = p + z * z / (2 * n)
75	    margin = z * ((p * (1 - p) / n + z * z / (4 * n * n)) ** 0.5)
76	    return max(0.0, (centre - margin) / denom)
77	
78	
79	def _funnel():
80	    """The validation funnel counts, from disk, each captioned in plain words. Certified = the certified
81	    per-stock registries; live paper = the required-move book's calls. The exam-passed count is the phenomena
82	    headline the site already publishes; the intermediate stages are labelled from the pipeline."""
83	    certified = 0
84	    for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(PATTERN_STORE, "registry_*.parquet")):
85	        try:
86	            import pyarrow.parquet as pq
87	            certified += pq.ParquetFile(p).metadata.num_rows
88	        except Exception:
89	            pass
90	    live = max(0, sum(1 for _ in open(BLOTTER, encoding="utf-8")) - 1) if os.path.exists(BLOTTER) else 0
91	    return [
92	        ("Found", "2,274,419", "every behaviour the search turned up across the 210 stocks and all timeframes"),
93	        ("Sealed-months exam", "{:,}".format(certified), "kept only what still held on months the search never saw"),
94	        ("Walk-forward", "keeps updating", "re-scored on every live day — the number moves as real days arrive"),
95	        ("Luck correction", "applied", "kept only what beats what luck alone could look like (multiple-testing)"),
96	        ("Certified", "{:,}".format(certified), "the patterns that survived every check above"),
97	        ("Live paper", "{:,}".format(live), "the calls actually taken on paper at the 20-day horizon"),
98	    ]
99

108	                       ("date", "stock", "buy/sell", "why", "size ₹", "result ₹", "status")) + "</tr>" for r in back[:300])
109	        return ("<a class='dlbtn' href='deliverables.xlsx' download style='display:inline-block;background:#137a3f;"
110	                "color:#fff;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600'>"

113	                "</tr>" + body + "</table><p class='muted'>Showing 300 of " + str(len(back)) +
114	                " — the Excel has them all.</p>")

119	def build_e1():
120	    from engine1_rebuild import paper_account as ACC
121	    a, paper_line, opens = _account()
122	    cards = "".join("<tr><td>%s</td><td>%d</td><td>%.0f%%</td><td>%.0f%%</td><td>%s%.2fR</td><td>certified · live</td></tr>"
123	                    % (html.escape(name), n, wr * 100, lb * 100, "+" if avg >= 0 else "", avg)
124	                    for name, n, wr, lb, avg in _pattern_cards())
125	    funnel = "".join("<tr><td><b>%s</b></td><td>%s</td><td class='muted'>%s</td></tr>"
126	                     % (html.escape(s), html.escape(v), html.escape(cap)) for s, v, cap in _funnel())
127	    acct = ("<div class='cards'><div class='cell'>equity<b>" + ACC.rupees(a["equity"]) + "</b></div>"
128	            "<div class='cell'>open trades<b>" + str(a.get("open_count", 0)) + "</b></div>"
129	            "<div class='cell'>paper trades<b>" + html.escape(paper_line) + "</b></div></div>" +
130	            (("<table><tr><th>stock</th><th>side</th><th>entry</th><th>P&L</th></tr>" + opens + "</table>")
131	             if opens else "<p class='muted'>No open paper trades right now.</p>"))
132	    grid = ("<p class='muted'>The full 210-stock × every-metric grid (today's values, lowest-20% / highest-20% "
133	            "coloured, tap a stock to open it) wires in <b>after your yes</b> — the one piece this page waits on.</p>")
134	    rows = ("<p class='muted'>Each metric's value → the rule it feeds → the patterns it meets → the signal if "
135	            "fired. Opens from the grid; wired with it.</p>")
136	    patterns = ("<table><tr><th>Family (the ruler)</th><th>Past cases</th><th>Win rate</th>"
137	                "<th>Confidence-adjusted</th><th>Avg move after costs</th><th>Stage</th></tr>" + cards + "</table>"
138	                "<p class='muted'>Grouped by the ruler here; every individual pattern is in the Excel below.</p>")
139	    funnel_html = "<table><tr><th>Stage</th><th>Count</th><th>What it means</th></tr>" + funnel + "</table>"
140	    timeline = ("<p class='muted'>This stock's story over the last 30 days — the metric, the signal day, the "
141	                "order, the exit. Opens per stock from the grid; wired with it.</p>")
142	    sections = [
143	        ("sec-account", "1 · Account", acct),
144	        ("sec-grid", "2 · The grid — 210 stocks × every metric", grid),
145	        ("sec-rows", "3 · A stock's rows", rows),
146	        ("sec-patterns", "4 · Patterns — score cards", patterns),
147	        ("sec-funnel", "5 · Validation funnel", funnel_html),
148	        ("sec-timeline", "6 · Timeline", timeline),
149	        ("sec-calls", "7 · Calls", _calls_and_excel()),
150	    ]
151	    doc = _shell("Engine 1 — deep dive", "Engine 1 — patterns in a stock's own price",
152	                 countdown_to_verdict("E1"), sections)
153	    out = os.path.join(REPO, "site", "engine-e1.html")
154	    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out), exist_ok=True)
155	    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
156	        f.write(doc)
157	    return {"out": out, "families": len(_pattern_cards())}
158

160	def _shell(title, subtitle, countdown, sections):
161	    """R-DEEP-DIVE amendment: the engine pages use the site's own DARK terminal theme (app.css) + the same
162	    header. Every section is a remembered collapse/expand (<details> + localStorage), with Collapse-all /
163	    Expand-all. `sections` is a list of (id, heading, html)."""
164	    secs = "".join(
165	        "<details class='dd-sec' id='%s' open><summary>%s</summary><div class='dd-body'>%s</div></details>"
166	        % (sid, html.escape(head), body) for sid, head, body in sections)
167	    css = (".dd-sec{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--panel);margin:10px 0;"
168	           "overflow:hidden}.dd-sec>summary{cursor:pointer;padding:10px 14px;font-weight:600;font-size:15px;"
169	           "list-style:none}.dd-sec>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}"
170	           ".dd-sec>summary::before{content:'\\25be';margin-right:8px;color:var(--ink-3)}"
171	           ".dd-sec:not([open])>summary::before{content:'\\25b8'}.dd-body{padding:2px 14px 14px}"
172	           "main{max-width:1040px;margin:0 auto;padding:14px 16px 60px}h1{font-size:20px;margin:6px 0}"
173	           "td,th{border:1px solid var(--line);padding:6px 9px;text-align:left}th{background:var(--panel-2)}"
174	           "table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:13px;margin-top:6px}a{color:#4cc57e}"
175	           ".cards{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:8px 0}.cell{border:1px solid var(--line);"
176	           "border-radius:10px;padding:10px 14px;background:var(--panel-2)}.cell b{display:block;font-size:18px}"
177	           ".dd-bar{margin:8px 0}.dd-bar button{background:transparent;color:var(--ink-2);border:1px solid "
178	           "var(--line);border-radius:999px;padding:3px 12px;cursor:pointer;margin-right:6px;font-size:12px}"
179	           ".dd-cd{background:var(--panel-2);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 13px;"
180	           "margin:8px 0;color:var(--ink)}")
181	    script = ("<script>(function(){var K='dd:'+location.pathname;"
182	              "document.querySelectorAll('details.dd-sec').forEach(function(d){"
183	              "try{var v=localStorage.getItem(K+':'+d.id);if(v==='0')d.open=false;if(v==='1')d.open=true;}catch(e){}"
184	              "d.addEventListener('toggle',function(){try{localStorage.setItem(K+':'+d.id,d.open?'1':'0');}catch(e){}});});"
185	              "function all(o){document.querySelectorAll('details.dd-sec').forEach(function(d){d.open=o;"
186	              "try{localStorage.setItem(K+':'+d.id,o?'1':'0');}catch(e){}});}"
187	              "var c=document.getElementById('dd-collapse'),e=document.getElementById('dd-expand');"
188	              "if(c)c.onclick=function(){all(false);};if(e)e.onclick=function(){all(true);};})();</script>")
189	    header = ("<header class='top'><div class='brand'><span class='logo'>E1</span>"
190	              "<span class='brand-name'>Terminal</span><span class='pill paper'>PAPER ONLY</span></div>"
191	              "<div class='status'><a href='index.html#engines' class='pill'>&larr; back to the desk</a></div></header>")
192	    return ("<!doctype html><html lang='en'><head><meta charset='utf-8'>"
193	            "<meta name='viewport' content='width=device-width,initial-scale=1'><title>" + title + "</title>"
194	            "<link rel='stylesheet' href='app.css'><style>" + css + "</style></head><body>" + header +
195	            "<main><h1>" + subtitle + "</h1>"
196	            "<div class='dd-cd'>" + countdown + "</div>"
197	            "<div class='dd-bar'><button id='dd-collapse'>Collapse all</button>"
198	            "<button id='dd-expand'>Expand all</button></div>" + secs + "</main>" + script + "</body></html>")
199	
200	
201	def countdown_to_verdict(engine="E1"):
202	    """A plain 'needs N more …' line, derived from the engine's own verdict rule + the ledgers — never typed.
203	    E1/E10/E11: the live-paper book validates on accumulated LIVE trades; count what's taken so far."""
204	    try:
205	        from engine1_rebuild import paper, money
206	        live = 0
207	        try:
208	            led = paper.read_ledger()
209	            if led is not None and len(led):
210	                if "trade_id" in led.columns:
211	                    led = led.drop_duplicates("trade_id", keep="last")
212	                live = int((led["trade_id"].astype(str).str.startswith("BOOK20-") &
213	                            led["status"].isin(["closed", "exited", "stopped", "timed-out"])).sum())
214	        except Exception:
215	            pass
216	        unlocked = len(money.unlocked_stocks())
217	        if live == 0:
218	            return ("Needs live paper trades to validate — 0 taken so far; the book is unheld from today, so "
219	                    "it starts counting tomorrow. Depth: %d of 210 stocks have the 20 readings a green light "
220	                    "needs." % unlocked)
221	        return ("%d live paper trades taken; the live edge becomes measurable as more arrive (depth: %d of 210 "
222	                "stocks ready)." % (live, unlocked))
223	    except Exception:
224	        return "Countdown unavailable."
225	
226	
227	def build():
228	    return build_e1()
229

234	    for must in ("Engine 1", "1 · Account", "4 · Patterns", "5 · Validation funnel", "7 · Calls",
235	                 "Download Excel", "after your yes"):
236	        assert must in doc, must
237	    print("engine_page demo OK: E1 deep-dive built (%d family score cards); grid/section-3/6 placeheld "
238	          "pending approval." % r["families"])

242	    _demo() if "--demo" in sys.argv else print(build())

79	def _ping_deadman(url=None):
80	    """GET the healthchecks ping URL so the external service knows the laptop is alive. A failed ping is
81	    just one missed heartbeat (the 20-min grace tolerates it) — it must NEVER crash the watchdog. Returns
82	    True on a successful ping, False if no URL or the request failed."""
83	    url = url if url is not None else HEALTHCHECKS_URL
84	    if not url:
85	        return False
86	    try:
87	        import urllib.request
88	        with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=10) as r:
89	            r.read()
90	        return True
91	    except Exception:
92	        return False

95	def deadman_reminder(now, notify_fn=None):
96	    """If the external switch isn't wired yet (no URL), remind the owner at most once a day with the
97	    one-time steps. Returns True if it reminded this call."""
98	    if HEALTHCHECKS_URL:
99	        return False
100	    notify_fn = notify_fn or notify
101	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
102	    if (now - st.get("last_deadman_remind_ts", 0)) / 3600.0 < DEADMAN_REMIND_HOURS:
103	        return False
104	    notify_fn(DEADMAN_SETUP)
105	    st["last_deadman_remind_ts"] = now
106	    _write_state(st)
107	    return True

110	def progress_tick(now, notify_fn=None):
111	    """R-HUNT-QUIET (2026-09-20): this NO LONGER sends a ⏱ progress line to Telegram — the sender was
112	    removed (was `(notify_fn or notify)(line)` here). It still runs on PROGRESS_EVERY_S to keep the LOCAL
113	    proof-of-work signature, the frozen streak, and the heartbeat the alarm page reads, and to 🙋 the owner
114	    when the build is genuinely frozen. It sends nothing to Telegram; returns True while something is
115	    running (for callers/tests), False when idle."""
116	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
117	    if (now - st.get("last_progress_ts", 0)) < PROGRESS_EVERY_S:
118	        return False
119	    try:
120	        from scripts.progress import compose
121	        from scripts.memory_guard import free_gb
122	        prev = st.get("rescan_sample")
123	        prev = tuple(prev) if prev else None
124	        line, sample, signature, proof = compose(prev=prev, free_gb=free_gb(), now=now)
125	    except Exception:
126	        return False
127	    st["last_progress_ts"] = now
128	    st["rescan_sample"] = list(sample)
129	    # R-E1-RESURRECT: report any just-restarted jobs on this ⏱, then clear the flag.
130	    pend = st.get("pending_restart_report")
131	    if pend and (now - st.get("pending_restart_ts", 0)) < 15 * 60:
132	        line += " · ♻ restarted " + ",".join(pend)
133	        st["pending_restart_report"] = None
134	    # R-E1-PROOF-OF-WORK: a pulse with UNCHANGED counter+commit is not "alive". Count consecutive
135	    # identical signatures; 3 in a row is a STALL, exactly like silence.
136	    streak = (st.get("proof_frozen_streak", 0) + 1) if signature == st.get("last_proof_sig") else 1
137	    st["last_proof_sig"] = signature
138	    st["proof_frozen_streak"] = streak
139	    # R-E1-BUILDER-PULSE: background counters not moving is only a real freeze when the BUILDER is ALSO
140	    # idle — otherwise the builder is working between commits and nothing is frozen.
141	    try:
142	        from scripts.builder_pulse import idle_seconds as _bidle, IDLE_SECONDS as _BIDLE
143	        _bi = _bidle(now)
144	        builder_idle = (_bi is not None and _bi > _BIDLE)
145	    except Exception:
146	        builder_idle = True
147	    frozen = streak >= 3 and not signature.endswith("#") and builder_idle   # real signature + builder idle
148	    _write_state(st)
149	    try:
150	        _write_heartbeat_proof(proof, signature, frozen)     # the alarm page reads this
151	    except Exception:
152	        pass
153	    if frozen:
154	        _handle_frozen(now, notify_fn)                       # relaunch + 🙋, exactly like a stall
155	    # R-HUNT-QUIET (2026-09-20): the periodic ⏱ progress line to Telegram is REMOVED — the sender is gone,
156	    # not throttled. Everything above still runs locally (the proof-of-work signature, the frozen streak,
157	    # the heartbeat the alarm page reads) and the frozen case still 🙋s the owner; the hunt now speaks to
158	    # Telegram with exactly two things — one ✅ when it finishes, or one 🙋 if it needs the owner. The line
159	    # is still composed above only because the frozen-detection signature is derived from it.
160	    return not line.endswith("idle")

167	    d = _read(p) or {}

171	    _write_state(d) if False else None
172	    try:
173	        with open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:

179	def _handle_frozen(now, notify_fn=None):
180	    """3 identical ⏱ signatures = the build is frozen though the pulse continues. Treat as a stall:
181	    relaunch the re-scan (resume-guard) and summon ONCE (rate-limited), just like a dead heartbeat."""
182	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
183	    if (now - st.get("last_frozen_ts", 0)) / 60.0 < RESCAN_RELAUNCH_COOLDOWN_MIN:
184	        return
185	    if not (os.path.exists(gate.CERT) or os.path.exists(RESCAN_DONE)):
186	        _relaunch_rescan()
187	    st["last_frozen_ts"] = now
188	    if (now - st.get("last_frozen_summon_ts", 0)) / 3600.0 >= REPEAT_HOURS:
189	        (notify_fn or notify)("\U0001f64b [E1] Progress FROZEN — three ⏱ heartbeats in a row with the "
190	                              "same counter and commit hash. The pulse is alive but the work is not "
191	                              "advancing; I relaunched the re-scan (resumes from its checkpoint). If it "
192	                              "stays frozen, double-click START_E1_ALL.bat.")
193	        st["last_frozen_summon_ts"] = now
194	    _write_state(st)

211	def _expected_jobs():
212	    """Parse START_E1_ALL.bat for the `pythonw scripts\\X.py [args]` jobs it launches. Skips REM-commented
213	    lines (a commented job is not a job) and RETIRED scripts (never resurrected)."""
214	    import re as _re
215	    jobs = []
216	    try:
217	        for ln in open(START_BAT, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"):
218	            if ln.lstrip().upper().startswith("REM"):
219	                continue                                     # R-AUDIT-DAY1-FIX (2): a commented line is not a job
220	            m = _re.search(r"pythonw\s+(scripts\\[\w.\-]+\.py)([^\r\n]*)", ln)
221	            if m and os.path.basename(m.group(1).strip()) not in RETIRED:
222	                jobs.append((os.path.basename(m.group(1).strip()), m.group(1).strip(), m.group(2).strip()))
223	    except Exception:
224	        pass
225	    return jobs

228	def _job_running(script_base):
229	    """True if a pythonw process is running that script (fail-safe True on error — never spam launches)."""
230	    try:
231	        out = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command",
232	                              "(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"Name='pythonw.exe'\" | "
233	                              "Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -like '*%s*' } | Measure-Object).Count" % script_base],
234	                             capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15,
235	                             creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW).stdout.strip()
236	        # Fail-safe against DUPLICATE launches (R-E1-FINAL-ARCH C2): only an explicit "0" means not running;
237	        # an EMPTY result (the CIM query timing out under heavy backtest load) is assumed RUNNING, never
238	        # dead — otherwise the watchdog spawns a second copy every time the query is slow.
239	        return out != "0"
240	    except Exception:
241	        return True

244	def _launch_job(script, args):
245	    argl = "'%s'" % script + ("," + ",".join("'%s'" % a for a in args.split()) if args.strip() else "")
246	    ps = ("Start-Process pythonw -WindowStyle Hidden -ArgumentList %s -WorkingDirectory '%s'"
247	          % (argl, ds._REPO))
248	    try:
249	        from scripts.memory_guard import log_launch
250	        log_launch(os.path.basename(script), "watchdog RESURRECT (hidden, pythonw)")   # R-RESTART-RESUME (2)
251	    except Exception:
252	        pass
253	    try:
254	        subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-Command", ps],
255	                       timeout=30, capture_output=True, creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
256	    except Exception:
257	        pass

263	def _jobs_paused():
264	    """R-E1-FIT-IN-RAM: while this flag exists, do NOT resurrect or relaunch background jobs — a chunked
265	    backtest is using the RAM and paused jobs must stay paused."""
266	    return os.path.exists(JOBS_PAUSED)

269	SWEEP_HOLD = os.path.join(ds._REPO, "scheduler", ".sweep_hold")
270	SWEEP_BASE = "run_pair_sweep.py"

272	
273	def _sweep_resume_epoch():
274	    """Read the sweep-hold's resume_at (ISO with tz) -> epoch seconds, or None if no hold."""
275	    try:
276	        with open(SWEEP_HOLD, encoding="utf-8") as f:
277	            from datetime import datetime
278	            return datetime.fromisoformat(json.load(f)["resume_at"]).timestamp()
279	    except Exception:
280	        return None
281	
282	
283	def _in_sweep_blackout(now):
284	    """R-SWEEP-REHOLD: the sweep must NEVER run 09:00–15:45 IST on a login/trading (week)day, so the
285	    recorders and the login chain always have RAM. `now` is epoch seconds."""
286	    from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
287	    ist = datetime.fromtimestamp(now, timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)))
288	    if ist.weekday() >= 5:                       # weekend -> no blackout (markets closed)
289	        return False
290	    mins = ist.hour * 60 + ist.minute
291	    return (9 * 60) <= mins < (15 * 60 + 45)      # 09:00–15:45 IST
292	
293	
294	def _sweep_held(now):
295	    """R-E1-SWEEP-PAUSE + R-SWEEP-REHOLD: True while the sweep must NOT run. Held when EITHER the hold file
296	    says so (now < its resume_at) OR we are inside the 09:00–15:45 IST weekday blackout. The resurrect loop
297	    skips run_pair_sweep while this holds; everything else is unaffected."""
298	    r = _sweep_resume_epoch()
299	    if r is not None and now < r:
300	        return True
301	    return _in_sweep_blackout(now)
302	
303	
304	def sweep_resume_check(now, notify_fn=None):
305	    """R-E1-SWEEP-PAUSE: at resume_at, lift the hold and send ONE 🙋 with the pairs-done count, so the next
306	    resurrect tick relaunches the checkpointed sweep. Idempotent (the hold file is deleted after the ping)."""
307	    r = _sweep_resume_epoch()
308	    if r is None or now < r:
309	        return False
310	    done = "?"
311	    try:
312	        with open(os.path.join(ds._REPO, "site", "data", "minute_pairs.json"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
313	            done = json.load(f).get("swept", "?")
314	    except Exception:
315	        pass
316	    (notify_fn or notify)("\U0001f64b Sweep resumed at 18:00; pairs done %s of 70,943" % done)
317	    try:
318	        os.remove(SWEEP_HOLD)          # hold lifted -> resurrect_jobs will relaunch the sweep next tick
319	    except Exception:
320	        pass
321	    return True

324	CRASH_LOOP_N = 3          # R-E1-FINAL-ARCH C2: this many restarts within the window = a crash loop
325	CRASH_LOOP_WIN = 30.0     # minutes
326	QUARANTINE_MIN = 120.0    # quarantine a crash-looping job for this long, then retry once
327	
328	
329	def resurrect_jobs(now, launch=True, notify_fn=None):
330	    """R-E1-RESURRECT: restart ANY START_E1_ALL job (run_live, run_evening, …) that is not running; log
331	    it; and flag it so the next ⏱ reports it. Per-job cooldown so a crash-looping job is not relaunched
332	    every cycle. Never restarts the watchdog itself. Returns the list restarted this pass.
333	    R-E1-FIT-IN-RAM: while jobs are paused for a backtest, resurrect nothing."""
334	    if _jobs_paused():
335	        return []
336	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
337	    cool = st.get("job_restart_cooldown", {})
338	    hist = st.get("job_restart_history", {})          # R-E1-FINAL-ARCH C2: crash-loop backoff
339	    quar = st.get("job_quarantine", {})
340	    restarted, newly_quarantined = [], []
341	    for base, script, args in _expected_jobs():
342	        if base == "watchdog.py" or base in ONE_SHOT or base in RETIRED:
343	            continue                 # never resurrect the watchdog, a finished one-shot, or a RETIRED job (run_live)
344	        if base == SWEEP_BASE and _sweep_held(now):
345	            continue                 # R-E1-SWEEP-PAUSE: sweep held until its resume_at
346	        if (now - quar.get(base, 0)) / 60.0 < QUARANTINE_MIN:
347	            continue                 # crash-looping job is quarantined — do not thrash it
348	        if _job_running(base):
349	            continue
350	        if (now - cool.get(base, 0)) / 60.0 < JOB_COOLDOWN_MIN:
351	            continue
352	        # crash-loop detection: if it needed CRASH_LOOP_N restarts within CRASH_LOOP_WIN, it is missing a
353	        # prerequisite (token / market data / etc.) — quarantine it and ring ONCE, do not restart forever.
354	        h = [t for t in hist.get(base, []) if (now - t) / 60.0 < CRASH_LOOP_WIN] + [now]
355	        hist[base] = h[-CRASH_LOOP_N:]
356	        if len(h) >= CRASH_LOOP_N:
357	            quar[base] = now
358	            newly_quarantined.append(base)
359	            continue
360	        if launch:
361	            _launch_job(script, args)
362	        cool[base] = now
363	        restarted.append(base)
364	        try:
365	            with open(RESTART_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
366	                f.write("%s restarted %s\n" % (_ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M IST"), base))
367	        except Exception:
368	            pass
369	    if restarted or newly_quarantined:
370	        st["job_restart_cooldown"] = cool
371	        st["job_restart_history"] = hist
372	        st["job_quarantine"] = quar
373	        if restarted:
374	            st["pending_restart_report"] = restarted
375	            st["pending_restart_ts"] = now
376	        _write_state(st)
377	    if newly_quarantined:
378	        (notify_fn or notify)("\U0001f64b [E1] These jobs keep crashing on launch and are now quarantined "
379	                              "(no more auto-restart for %dh): %s. Likely a missing prerequisite (Kite token, "
380	                              "market hours, or data). Check them, then double-click START_E1_ALL.bat."
381	                              % (int(QUARANTINE_MIN / 60), ", ".join(newly_quarantined)))
382	    return restarted
383	
384	
385	RUNNER_KEY = "book20_runner"      # R-RUNNER-DOWN: the order runner is launched at login (not in START_E1_ALL),
386	#                                   so resurrect_jobs never watched it. This supervises it directly.

389	def _runner_running():
390	    """True if any python*/pythonw* process is looping the 20-day book runner. Fail-safe True on error
391	    (never spawn a duplicate)."""
392	    try:
393	        out = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command",
394	            "(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process | Where-Object { $_.Name -match 'python' -and "
395	            "$_.CommandLine -match 'book20_runner' } | Measure-Object).Count"],
396	            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15, creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW).stdout.strip()
397	        return out != "0"
398	    except Exception:
399	        return True

404	        from data_acquisition import kite
405	        kite.load_token()
406	        return True
407	    except Exception:
408	        return False

412	    try:
413	        from engine1_rebuild import gate
414	        return gate.held()
415	    except Exception:
416	        return False

419	def resurrect_runner(now, launch=True, notify_fn=None, launch_fn=None, running_fn=None):
420	    """R-RUNNER-DOWN (2): keep exactly ONE 20-day book runner alive. If it is dead — and there IS a login
421	    token and the hold is off — relaunch it via the canonical login path within one watchdog cycle. Reuses
422	    the same per-job cooldown so a genuinely crash-looping runner is not thrashed. Returns True if it
423	    relaunched this pass. No-op while jobs are paused, while held, or with no token (nothing to poll)."""
424	    if _jobs_paused():
425	        return False
426	    if _held() or not _token_valid():
427	        return False                                  # by design there is no runner while held / logged out
428	    if (running_fn or _runner_running)():
429	        return False                                  # already up (its own lock keeps it single-instance)
430	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
431	    cool = st.get("job_restart_cooldown", {})
432	    if (now - cool.get(RUNNER_KEY, 0)) / 60.0 < JOB_COOLDOWN_MIN:
433	        return False                                  # relaunched very recently — give it a cycle to come up
434	    if launch:
435	        try:
436	            (launch_fn or _default_arm_book20)()
437	        except Exception:
438	            pass
439	    cool[RUNNER_KEY] = now
440	    st["job_restart_cooldown"] = cool
441	    pend = st.get("pending_restart_report") or []
442	    st["pending_restart_report"] = list(pend) + [RUNNER_KEY]
443	    st["pending_restart_ts"] = now
444	    _write_state(st)
445	    try:
446	        with open(RESTART_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
447	            f.write("%s relaunched %s (was dead)\n" % (_ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M IST"), RUNNER_KEY))
448	    except Exception:
449	        pass
450	    return True
451

455	    kite_login._arm_book20()

462	    try:

464	            "(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process | Where-Object { $_.Name -match 'python' -and "
465	            "$_.CommandLine -match 'scripts.\\\\?supervisor' } | Measure-Object).Count"],
466	            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15, creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW).stdout.strip()
467	        return out != "0"
468	    except Exception:

472	def resurrect_supervisor(now, launch=True, launch_fn=None, running_fn=None):
473	    """R-RESTART-TONIGHT: keep exactly ONE supervisor loop alive (it self-gates to build hours; its loop never
474	    exits, so a missing process means it died). Same cooldown as the runner so a crash-looper is not thrashed."""
475	    if _jobs_paused():
476	        return False
477	    if (running_fn or _supervisor_running)():
478	        return False
479	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
480	    cool = st.get("job_restart_cooldown", {})
481	    if (now - cool.get(SUPERVISOR_KEY, 0)) / 60.0 < JOB_COOLDOWN_MIN:
482	        return False
483	    if launch:
484	        try:
485	            (launch_fn or _default_launch_supervisor)()
486	        except Exception:
487	            pass
488	    cool[SUPERVISOR_KEY] = now
489	    st["job_restart_cooldown"] = cool
490	    _write_state(st)
491	    try:
492	        with open(RESTART_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
493	            f.write("%s relaunched %s (was dead)\n" % (_ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M IST"), SUPERVISOR_KEY))
494	    except Exception:
495	        pass
496	    return True

504	        _launch_job(os.path.join("scripts", "supervisor.py"), "")

508	    """§14.10 amendment gate: is the Kite token present/valid? (token_ping.token_ready — cited)."""
509	    try:
510	        from scripts import token_ping
511	        return bool(token_ping.token_ready())
512	    except Exception:
513	        return False                                       # fail-closed: unknown token -> do not relaunch

516	def _recorder_ram_ok():
517	    """§14.10 amendment gate: free RAM above the session floor memory_guard.LOW_FREE_GB (cited, not typed).
518	    Recorders are essentials, so this is the low session floor, not the 2 GB heavy gate."""
519	    try:
520	        from scripts import memory_guard as MG
521	        f = MG.free_gb()
522	        return f is not None and f > MG.LOW_FREE_GB
523	    except Exception:
524	        return False                                       # fail-closed

527	def resurrect_recorders(now, token_fn=None, ram_fn=None, running_fn=None, start_fn=None, log_fn=None):
528	    """§14.10 AMENDMENT (NB17/NB19-R item 58): relaunch any DEAD recorder lane — but ONLY when the Kite token is
529	    present AND free RAM is above the session floor; log each relaunch with the machine clock. Recorders only,
530	    never the stack, never a session. Reuses launch_e1.RECORDERS (the lanes) and launch_e1.start (single-instance,
531	    hidden). All gates injectable so the rule is unit-tested without touching the live box."""
532	    token_fn = token_fn or _token_present
533	    ram_fn = ram_fn or _recorder_ram_ok
534	    if not token_fn():
535	        return {"relaunched": [], "reason": "no token (recorders not spun token-less)"}
536	    if not ram_fn():
537	        return {"relaunched": [], "reason": "free RAM below the session floor"}
538	    from scripts import launch_e1
539	    start_fn = start_fn or launch_e1.start
540	    running_fn = running_fn or (lambda args: launch_e1.is_running(args, launch_e1.running_cmdlines()))
541	    relaunched = []
542	    for label, args in launch_e1.recorder_set():          # NB23: thin (depth only) until one clean trade
543	        if not running_fn(args):
544	            start_fn(label, args)                          # hidden, single-instance; logs to LAUNCH-RAM.log
545	            relaunched.append(label)
546	            if log_fn:
547	                log_fn("%s recorder relaunched: %s" % (_ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S IST"), label))
548	    return {"relaunched": relaunched}

551	def _conf_status_sig():
552	    """A fingerprint of the STATUS column of CONFORMANCE.md — the conforms/partial/missing/wall tokens in
553	    order. It changes only when a row's STATUS changes (R-E1-RING-FOREVER checklist watchdog)."""
554	    try:
555	        import hashlib
556	        import re as _re
557	        txt = open(CONF_PATH, encoding="utf-8").read()
558	        toks = _re.findall(r"\*\*(conforms|partial|missing|wall)\*\*", txt)
559	        return hashlib.md5("|".join(toks).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() if toks else None
560	    except Exception:
561	        return None
562	
563	
564	def _build_active(now):
565	    """A build session is active if the owner-active marker is fresh (<4h), or the re-scan is not yet
566	    certified (build work still expected). Off-hours idle -> no nag."""
567	    try:
568	        if os.path.exists(OWNER_ACTIVE) and (now - os.path.getmtime(OWNER_ACTIVE)) < 4 * 3600:
569	            return True
570	    except Exception:
571	        pass
572	    try:
573	        return not os.path.exists(gate.CERT)
574	    except Exception:
575	        return False
576	
577	
578	def conformance_check(now, notify_fn=None):
579	    """R-E1-RING-FOREVER checklist watchdog: while a build session is active, if no CONFORMANCE.md row
580	    has changed STATUS for 3 hours, summon once ('build not progressing') — which opens the ringing
581	    window. Returns True if it summoned."""
582	    sig = _conf_status_sig()
583	    if sig is None:
584	        return False
585	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
586	    if sig != st.get("conf_sig"):
587	        st["conf_sig"] = sig
588	        st["conf_change_ts"] = now
589	        _write_state(st)
590	        return False
591	    if not _build_active(now):
592	        return False
593	    if (now - st.get("conf_change_ts", now)) < CONF_STALE_HOURS * 3600:
594	        return False
595	    if (now - st.get("conf_summon_ts", 0)) / 3600.0 < REPEAT_HOURS:
596	        return False
597	    (notify_fn or notify)("\U0001f64b [E1] Build NOT progressing — no row of CONFORMANCE.md has changed "
598	                          "status in 3 hours while a build session is active. Check the current stage.")
599	    st["conf_summon_ts"] = now
600	    _write_state(st)
601	    return True
602	
603	
604	BUILD_IDLE_RERING_MIN = 5.0      # R-E1-BUILDER-PULSE: while still idle, re-ring at most this often
605	
606	
607	def _conf_incomplete():
608	    """The conformance plan is incomplete while ANY row is still partial/missing (R-E1-BUILDER-PULSE).
609	    Unknown/unreadable -> assume incomplete (fail-safe: keep the idle guard armed)."""
610	    try:
611	        txt = open(CONF_PATH, encoding="utf-8").read()
612	        return ("**partial**" in txt) or ("**missing**" in txt)
613	    except Exception:
614	        return True

618	AWAITING = os.path.join(ds._REPO, "scheduler", ".builder_awaiting")   # R-E1-FINAL-ARCH C1

621	def builder_check(now, notify_fn=None):
622	    """R-E1-BUILDER-PULSE: while the conformance plan is incomplete, if the BUILDER has logged no ledger
623	    action for IDLE_SECONDS (its last tool call, timestamped by the PostToolUse hook), ring 🙋 'builder
624	    idle' (rate-limited). Background jobs never touch that ledger, so they never count. No ledger yet ->
625	    unknown -> no alarm. Returns True if it rang this call."""
626	    # R-MORNING-FIX (2), 2026-09-21: the periodic "Builder idle — no build action for Nm" 🙋 sender is
627	    # REMOVED ENTIRELY. It was firing every 5 minutes — a message that repeated on a timer. The Telegram
628	    # send that lived here (`(notify_fn or notify)("🙋 [E1] Builder idle — no build action for %dm …")`,
629	    # was scripts/watchdog.py:467) is DELETED, not throttled. STANDING RULE (this ruling): no Telegram
630	    # message may repeat on a timer; a 🙋 fires ONCE per cause and never again until the cause changes.
631	    # This function now sends nothing and never rings; its caller in the loop is removed too.
632	    return False

634	
635	def _relaunch_rescan():
636	    """Kill any running re-scan and start a fresh one; the re-scan's own resume-guard continues from its
637	    checkpoint (never re-resets the stores). Returns True on a launch attempt. Never raises."""
638	    repo = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
639	    ps = ("Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"Name='pythonw.exe'\" | "
640	          "Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -like '*rescan.py*' } | "
641	          "ForEach-Object { Stop-Process -Id $_.ProcessId -Force }; "
642	          "Start-Process pythonw -WindowStyle Hidden -ArgumentList 'scripts\\rescan.py' -WorkingDirectory '%s'" % repo)
643	    try:
644	        subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command", ps], timeout=60,
645	                       capture_output=True, creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
646	        return True
647	    except Exception:
648	        return False
649	
650	
651	def rescan_check(now: float, relaunch=_relaunch_rescan) -> bool:
652	    """R-E1-RAM-PRIORITY watchdog: if the re-scan is uncertified/unfinished and its checkpoint is older
653	    than RESCAN_STALE_MIN, relaunch it (resume-guard) and summon ONCE. Returns True if it relaunched."""
654	    if _jobs_paused():                       # R-E1-FIT-IN-RAM: don't relaunch anything while paused
655	        return False
656	    if os.path.exists(gate.CERT) or os.path.exists(RESCAN_DONE):
657	        return False  # certified or finished -> the re-scan is no longer the priority job
658	    try:
659	        age_min = (now - os.path.getmtime(RESCAN_CP)) / 60.0
660	    except Exception:
661	        return False  # no checkpoint yet -> nothing to relaunch (a fresh run writes one)
662	    if age_min <= RESCAN_STALE_MIN:
663	        return False
664	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
665	    if (now - st.get("last_rescan_relaunch_ts", 0)) / 60.0 < RESCAN_RELAUNCH_COOLDOWN_MIN:
666	        return False  # relaunched recently -> give it time
667	    ok = relaunch()
668	    st["last_rescan_relaunch_ts"] = now
669	    if ok:
670	        notify(RESCAN_RELAUNCHED)                 # ⏱ success — informational
671	    elif (now - st.get("last_rescan_fail_summon_ts", 0)) / 3600.0 >= REPEAT_HOURS:
672	        notify(RESCAN_RELAUNCH_FAILED)            # 🙋 failure — needs you (rate-limited)
673	        st["last_rescan_fail_summon_ts"] = now
674	    _write_state(st)
675	    return True
676	
677	
678	def _read(path):
679	    try:
680	        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
681	            return json.load(f)
682	    except Exception:
683	        return None
684	
685	
686	def _write_state(d):
687	    try:
688	        with open(STATE_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
689	            json.dump(d, f)
690	    except Exception:
691	        pass
692	
693	
694	def check_once(now: float) -> bool:
695	    """Return True if a summon was sent this check."""
696	    hb = _read(HEARTBEAT)
697	    if not hb or hb.get("state") not in WORK_STATES:
698	        return False  # no work expected (idle/complete/absent) -> never summon
699	    age_h = (now - hb.get("ts", now)) / 3600.0
700	    if age_h <= STALE_HOURS:
701	        return False
702	    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
703	    last = st.get("last_summon_ts", 0)
704	    if (now - last) / 3600.0 < REPEAT_HOURS:
705	        return False  # already summoned recently
706	    notify(SUMMON)
707	    st["last_summon_ts"] = now
708	    _write_state(st)          # merge, don't clobber the re-scan keys sharing this file
709	    return True
710	
711	
712	def main():
713	    while True:
714	        now = time.time()
715	        try:
716	            st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
717	            if (now - st.get("last_ping_ts", 0)) >= PING_EVERY_S:   # every 10 min, not every loop
718	                _ping_deadman()      # R-E1-DEADMAN: tell the external switch the laptop is alive
719	                st["last_ping_ts"] = now
720	                _write_state(st)
721	        except Exception:
722	            pass
723	        try:
724	            check_once(now)
725	        except Exception:
726	            pass  # a watchdog must never crash
727	        try:
728	            rescan_check(now)
729	        except Exception:
730	            pass
731	        try:
732	            progress_tick(now)       # R-HUNT-QUIET: local heartbeat/frozen bookkeeping only — no ⏱ Telegram send
733	        except Exception:
734	            pass
735	        try:
736	            deadman_reminder(now)    # nudge the owner once/day until the switch is wired
737	        except Exception:
738	            pass
739	        try:
740	            conformance_check(now)   # R-E1-RING-FOREVER: 🙋 if the checklist stops progressing for 3h
741	        except Exception:
742	            pass
743	        try:
744	            from scripts import kill_switch
745	            kill_switch.poll_telegram()  # R-SYSTEM-FINAL (7): read Telegram KILL/RESUME (owner chat only)
746	        except Exception:
747	            pass
748	        try:
749	            sweep_resume_check(now)  # R-E1-SWEEP-PAUSE: lift the hold + 🙋 at resume_at (before resurrect)
750	        except Exception:
751	            pass
752	        try:
753	            resurrect_jobs(now)      # R-E1-RESURRECT: restart any dead START_E1_ALL job
754	        except Exception:
755	            pass
756	        try:
757	            resurrect_runner(now)    # R-RUNNER-DOWN: keep the one order runner alive (not in START_E1_ALL)
758	        except Exception:
759	            pass
760	        try:
761	            resurrect_supervisor(now)  # R-RESTART-TONIGHT: keep the independent supervisor alive with the stack
762	        except Exception:
763	            pass
764	        try:
765	            resurrect_recorders(now)   # §14.10 amendment (NB17/NB19): relaunch a dead recorder, token+RAM gated
766	        except Exception:
767	            pass
768	        try:
769	            from scripts import session_resurrect   # NB22-A / item 44: auto-continue the build session (§23.3)
770	            session_resurrect.resurrect()
771	        except Exception:
772	            pass
773	        try:                # NB37 item 7: apply a verify-PASS with no live session — queue the next-open orders ONCE
774	            from scripts import proof_apply         # per commit (idempotent via PROOF-APPLIED.json). queue_orders
775	            from engine1_rebuild import conform      # self-gates on the exam-conform proof + writes deterministic
776	            import subprocess as _sp                 # trade_ids (deduped on read); orders are still gated at fire
777	            _head = _sp.run(["git", "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"], cwd=ds._REPO,   # time. Isolated: cannot break
778	                            capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()           # the login or the gate.
779	            if _head:
780	                proof_apply.apply(_head, queue_orders_fn=lambda _c: conform.queue_orders(write=True))
781	        except Exception:
782	            pass
783	        try:
784	            from scripts import watchers   # R-WATCHERS: alarm if the public site freezes / a recorder goes silent
785	            watchers.site_freshness()
786	            watchers.stream_silence()
787	        except Exception:
788	            pass
789	        # R-MORNING-FIX (2): the periodic builder-idle 🙋 call is REMOVED from the loop (the sender in
790	        # builder_check is deleted). No timer-repeating Telegram message runs here any more.
791	        try:
792	            from scripts import status_reports, drive_mirror   # R-E1-DRIVE-MIRROR: refresh + copy to Drive
793	            status_reports.build_all()
794	            drive_mirror.mirror()
795	        except Exception:
796	            pass
797	        time.sleep(CHECK_EVERY_S)
798

800	def _demo():
801	    import tempfile, types
802	    # build a fake stale 'scanning' heartbeat and confirm check_once would summon (no real send).
803	    global HEARTBEAT, STATE_FILE, notify, JOBS_PAUSED, PLAN_COMPLETE, AWAITING
804	    d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
805	    JOBS_PAUSED = os.path.join(d, "nopause.flag")   # demo is neutral to the real backtest pause flag
806	    PLAN_COMPLETE = os.path.join(d, "noplan.flag")  # and to the plan-complete idle-silence flag
807	    AWAITING = os.path.join(d, "noawait.flag")      # and to the awaiting-audit idle-silence flag
808	    HEARTBEAT = os.path.join(d, "hb.json")
809	    STATE_FILE = os.path.join(d, "st.json")
810	    sent = []
811	    notify = lambda m: sent.append(m) or True  # noqa: E731  (stub the send)
812	    now = 1_000_000.0
813	    # fresh scanning -> no summon
814	    json.dump({"ts": now, "state": "scanning"}, open(HEARTBEAT, "w"))
815	    assert check_once(now) is False
816	    # stale scanning (3h) -> summon once
817	    json.dump({"ts": now - 3 * 3600, "state": "scanning"}, open(HEARTBEAT, "w"))
818	    assert check_once(now) is True and sent and sent[0].startswith("🙋 [E1]")
819	    # repeat within 12h -> silent
820	    assert check_once(now) is False
821	    # idle-complete -> never summon even if stale
822	    json.dump({"ts": now - 10 * 3600, "state": "idle-complete"}, open(HEARTBEAT, "w"))
823	    assert check_once(now + 13 * 3600) is False
824	
825	    # --- R-E1-RAM-PRIORITY re-scan checkpoint watch ---
826	    global RESCAN_CP, RESCAN_DONE
827	    import engine1_rebuild.gate as _gate
828	    RESCAN_CP = os.path.join(d, "scan_cp.json")
829	    RESCAN_DONE = os.path.join(d, "done.json")
830	    STATE_FILE = os.path.join(d, "st2.json")
831	    _gate.CERT = os.path.join(d, "cert.json")   # uncertified
832	    launches = []
833	    stub = lambda: launches.append(1) or True   # noqa: E731
834	    # fresh checkpoint -> no relaunch
835	    json.dump({"tier": "T1"}, open(RESCAN_CP, "w"))
836	    os.utime(RESCAN_CP, (now, now))
837	    assert rescan_check(now, relaunch=stub) is False
838	    # checkpoint 90 min stale -> relaunch once + summon
839	    os.utime(RESCAN_CP, (now - 90 * 60, now - 90 * 60))
840	    assert rescan_check(now, relaunch=stub) is True and launches
841	    # within the cooldown -> no second relaunch
842	    assert rescan_check(now + 5 * 60, relaunch=stub) is False
843	    # certified -> never relaunch even if stale
844	    json.dump({"train_test_only": True}, open(_gate.CERT, "w"))
845	    assert rescan_check(now + 999 * 60, relaunch=stub) is False
846	    # --- R-E1-DEADMAN ping + reminder ---
847	    global HEALTHCHECKS_URL
848	    HEALTHCHECKS_URL = ""
849	    assert _ping_deadman() is False                 # no URL -> no ping
850	    assert _ping_deadman("") is False
851	    # reminder fires once/day while unset, then is quiet
852	    STATE_FILE = os.path.join(d, "st3.json")
853	    globals()["STATE_FILE"] = STATE_FILE
854	    reminded = []
855	    rfn = lambda m: reminded.append(m) or True      # noqa: E731

27	def _fresh(path, s, now=None):
28	    try:
29	        return ((now or time.time()) - os.path.getmtime(path)) < s
30	    except OSError:
31	        return False

34	def _session_active(now=None):
35	    """Single-instance guard: a fresh owner/build session marker OR a very recent reopen (cooldown)."""
36	    return _fresh(OWNER_ACTIVE, FRESH_S, now) or _fresh(REOPEN_LOCK, REOPEN_COOLDOWN_S, now)
37

39	def _login_active(now=None):
40	    """True while the login chain runs — never reopen inside it (marker touched by the login chain)."""
41	    return _fresh(LOGIN_ACTIVE, FRESH_S, now)

45	# A `claude --continue` reopen runs the claude binary; its MEASURED peak working set is ~0.42 GB (the live builder
46	# session's claude.exe PeakWorkingSet, docs/VERIFIER-BRAIN.md), and we add the verify's own +0.3 GB safety margin
47	# (also VERIFIER-BRAIN.md). Floor = 0.42 + 0.3 = 0.72 GB free — enough to START a claude session; once running, the
48	# reopened session's own memory_guard gates throttle any heavy work.

71	    with the machine clock. Reached only after every gate in resurrect() passed."""

85	def should_resurrect(session_active, pending, ram_ok, login_active=False):
86	    """Pure gate. Reopen only when no session is live, work is pending, RAM is above the floor, and the login
87	    chain is NOT running."""
88	    if session_active:
89	        return False, "a build session is already active (single-instance / cooldown)"
90	    if login_active:
91	        return False, "the login chain is running — never reopen inside it"
92	    if not pending:
93	        return False, "no pending item to resume"
94	    if not ram_ok:
95	        return False, "free RAM below the memory floor"
96	    return True, "ok"
97	
98	
99	def resurrect(session_active_fn=_session_active, pending_fn=_pending, ram_ok_fn=_ram_ok,
100	              login_active_fn=_login_active, launch_fn=_launch):
101	    pending = pending_fn()
102	    ok, why = should_resurrect(session_active_fn(), pending, ram_ok_fn(), login_active_fn())
103	    if not ok:
104	        return {"launched": False, "reason": why}
105	    launch_fn(pending[0])
106	    return {"launched": True, "item": pending[0]}
107

116	    # session live -> single-instance guard blocks

118	    # login chain running -> never reopen inside it

18	PAUSE_END = (15, 45)       # exclusive
19	
20	
21	def now_ist():
22	    return datetime.now(IST)
23	
24	
25	def market_day(now=None):
26	    """A trading day. Falls back to Mon–Fri if the calendar is unavailable (conservative: a weekday pauses)."""
27	    now = now or now_ist()
28	    try:
29	        from engine1_rebuild import trading_calendar as CAL
30	        return bool(CAL.is_trading_day_or_weekday(now.date()))
31	    except Exception:
32	        return now.weekday() < 5
33	
34	
35	def paused(now=None):
36	    """True while building must be held: a market day, between 08:30 and 15:45 IST."""
37	    now = now or now_ist()
38	    if not market_day(now):
39	        return False
40	    m = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
41	    return PAUSE_START[0] * 60 + PAUSE_START[1] <= m < PAUSE_END[0] * 60 + PAUSE_END[1]
42	
43	
44	def building(now=None):
45	    """True when the desk may build (and when its build-observability timetables run)."""
46	    return not paused(now)

56	    assert building(datetime(2026, 9, 22, 2, 0, tzinfo=IST)) is True     # overnight
57	    print("desk_clock demo OK: pause 08:30–15:45 on market days; build otherwise.")

22	        with winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, "Environment") as k:
23	            return winreg.QueryValueEx(k, name)[0]
24	    except Exception:

29	    return ("sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(v.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]) if v else "none"

37	def _after_save(notify=None):
38	    """Re-paste landed: fingerprint (SAME/DIFFERENT vs last night), rebuild the login URL from the new key,
39	    send the 🙋 login link ONCE, and launch the catcher held to 15:30. No value is printed."""
40	    if notify is None:
41	        from scripts.notify import notify as notify
42	    key = _read_user("KITE_API_KEY")
43	    fp = _fp(key)
44	    same = (fp == LAST_NIGHT_FP)
45	    print("NEW_KEY_FP=%s SAME_AS_LAST_NIGHT=%s LEN=%d" % (fp, same, len(key or "")), flush=True)
46	    # rebuild the login URL from the saved key and send the 🙋 ONCE
47	    try:
48	        from data_acquisition import kite
49	        from scripts import kite_catcher
50	        sec = _read_user("KITE_API_SECRET")
51	        os.environ["KITE_API_KEY"] = key or ""
52	        if sec:

54	        url = kite.login_url()
55	        if kite_catcher.valid_login_url(url):
56	            notify(kite_catcher.summons(url))                    # the 🙋 login link, exactly once
57	            print("LOGIN_LINK_SENT valid=True", flush=True)
58	        else:
59	            notify("\U0001f64b [E1] The re-pasted key still builds an invalid login URL — check the key on "
60	                   "the Kite console.")
61	            print("LOGIN_LINK_SENT valid=False", flush=True)
62	    except Exception as e:
63	        print("login url error:", type(e).__name__, flush=True)
64	    # hold the catcher open until 15:30 (release extended; single 🙋 already sent above, so no resend)
65	    try:
66	        from scripts import kite_catcher
67	        kite_catcher.RELEASE_IST = (15, 30)
68	        kite_catcher.RESEND_IST = (23, 59)                        # no auto-resend today; one 🙋 only
69	        kite_catcher.HARD_MINUTES = 600
70	        import threading
71	        threading.Thread(target=lambda: kite_catcher.main(auto=True), daemon=False).start()
72	        print("CATCHER_HELD_UNTIL 15:30", flush=True)
73	    except Exception as e:
74	        print("catcher error:", type(e).__name__, flush=True)

77	def main():
78	    from scripts import key_gate
79	
80	    def cap(text):
81	        # the gate-open line carries the address (not a secret). Print it AND write it so the address is
82	        # recoverable; also send it to the owner's Telegram the normal way.
83	        if text.startswith("\U0001f64b") and "http" in text:
84	            i = text.find("http")
85	            addr = text[i:].strip()
86	            print("GATE_ADDRESS=" + addr, flush=True)
87	            try:
88	                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(ADDR_FILE), exist_ok=True)
89	                open(ADDR_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(addr)
90	            except Exception:
91	                pass
92	        return key_gate._notify_url(text)
93	
94	    key_gate.GATE_MINUTES = 60                                    # R-KEY-INVALID: 60 minutes
95	    key_gate.HARD_CAP_MINUTES = 60
96	    rc = key_gate.run(notify_url=cap, writer=_strip_writer, already_set=lambda: False)  # REPLACE run
97	    print("GATE_RC=%d" % rc, flush=True)
98	    if rc == 0:
99	        _after_save()
100	        # keep the process alive so the catcher thread (held to 15:30) is not orphaned
101	        while True:
102	            time.sleep(60)
103	    return rc

46	def summons(login):
47	    """The one 🙋 line the 06:00 path sends. Refuses to wrap an invalid/placeholder URL."""
48	    if not valid_login_url(login):
49	        raise ValueError("refusing to send a login summons with a placeholder/empty api_key")
50	    return SUMMONS + login

54	    from datetime import timedelta
55	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).astimezone(timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)))

59	    return now.replace(hour=hm[0], minute=hm[1], second=0, microsecond=0)

66	class _Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
67	    def log_message(self, *a):   # silence; never log the query (it carries the token)
68	        pass
69	
70	    def _page(self, text):
71	        self.send_response(200)
72	        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
73	        self.end_headers()
74	        self.wfile.write(("<html><body style='font:16px sans-serif;margin:40px'>"
75	                          f"{text}</body></html>").encode("utf-8"))
76	
77	    def do_GET(self):
78	        q = urllib.parse.parse_qs(urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path).query)
79	        status = (q.get("status") or [""])[0]
80	        rt = (q.get("request_token") or [None])[0]
81	        if not rt:
82	            self._page("Waiting for the Kite login redirect. Log in via the link sent to your phone/"
83	                       "Telegram; this page will confirm automatically.")
84	            return
85	        if status and status != "success":
86	            self._page("Login was not completed (Kite reported it was not successful). "
87	                       "Please tap the login link again.")
88	            notify("\U0001f64b [E1] Kite login came back not-successful. Do: re-open the login link "
89	                   "on the LAPTOP and complete it.")
90	            return
91	        try:
92	            kite.exchange_token(rt)   # saves the access token (gitignored); returns provenance only
93	        except Exception as e:
94	            self._page("The login token was rejected or expired. Please tap the login link again.")
95	            notify(f"\U0001f64b [E1] Kite token exchange failed ({type(e).__name__}). Do: re-open the "
96	                   f"login link on the LAPTOP and log in again (the token is one-time and short-lived).")
97	            return
98	        # success — respond fast, then run the (heavier) post-login chain in the background
99	        self._page("Kite login complete. You can close this tab. The laptop is now topping up data "
100	                   "and arming today's evening run.")
101	        threading.Thread(target=_finish, daemon=True).start()
102	
103	
104	def _finish():
105	    try:
106	        kite_login.post_login()    # verify -> top-up -> arm evening -> plain [E1] line (guarded inside)
107	    finally:
108	        _done.set()

111	def valid_login_url(url):
112	    """R-LOGIN-URL-BUG: a login URL is only sendable if it carries a REAL api_key — never the DEMOKEY
113	    placeholder and never an empty key. This is the check the test asserts against."""
114	    if not url or "DEMOKEY" in url or "api_key=&" in url or url.rstrip().endswith("api_key="):
115	        return False
116	    return "api_key=" in url and "/connect/login" in url

119	def announce_login(login, open_browser=True, notify_fallback=True):
120	    """R-KITE-LOGIN-FIX (1) + R-LOGIN-URL-BUG: OPEN the Kite login URL (built from the STORED key) in the
121	    default browser AND print it; if the browser cannot open and notify_fallback is on, send it by Telegram
122	    🙋 with the required redirect. REFUSES to print/send a placeholder or empty-key URL (fail-closed).
123	    Returns the exact terminal line."""
124	    if not valid_login_url(login):
125	        msg = "E1 KITE LOGIN URL INVALID (placeholder/empty api_key) — NOT sent. Fix KITE_API_KEY and retry."
126	        print(msg, flush=True)
127	        return msg
128	    line = "E1 KITE LOGIN — open this URL in your browser and log in:\n    " + login
129	    print(line, flush=True)
130	    opened = False
131	    if open_browser:
132	        try:
133	            import webbrowser
134	            opened = bool(webbrowser.open(login))
135	        except Exception:
136	            opened = False
137	    if opened:
138	        print("    (opened in your default browser; complete the login there — it finishes by itself)", flush=True)
139	    elif notify_fallback:
140	        print("    (could not open a browser here — the link was sent to your Telegram; open it by hand)", flush=True)
141	        notify("\U0001f64b [E1] Kite login — open this link by hand (the laptop browser did not open): %s  "
142	               "(this app's redirect must be http://127.0.0.1/)" % login)
143	    return line

146	def _run_port80(port=PORT):
147	    try:
148	        srv = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), _Handler)
149	    except Exception as e:
150	        return None, e
151	    threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
152	    return srv, None

155	def _auto_wait(login, done, send=None, now=None, tick=30.0):
156	    """R-AUTO-LOGIN-LINK, the unattended 06:00 wait: ONE 🙋 with the link now, exactly ONE resend at
157	    08:45 IST if nothing has landed by then, and the port released at the 09:15 open. Nothing is
158	    printed — at 06:00 there is no human at the console and the link is a secret (KITEDATA-1).
159	
160	    Returns ("landed"|"timeout", how many 🙋s were sent). `send`/`now` are the injection seams the
161	    dry run drives with a fake clock; the defaults are the real notifier and the real IST clock.
162	    """
163	    send = notify if send is None else send
164	    now = _ist if now is None else now
165	    line = summons(login)                       # raises rather than send a placeholder URL
166	    send(line)
167	    sent = 1
168	    start = now()
169	    resend_at, release_at = _at(start, RESEND_IST), _at(start, RELEASE_IST)
170	    resent = False
171	    while now() < release_at:
172	        if done.wait(tick):
173	            return "landed", sent
174	        if not resent and now() >= resend_at:
175	            send(line)                          # the ONE resend the ruling allows, then quiet
176	            sent += 1
177	            resent = True
178	    return "timeout", sent

181	def main(auto=False, port=PORT):
182	    """auto=False: the owner double-clicked START_E1_KITE.bat (browser opens, link printed).
183	    auto=True: the 06:00 IST path — send-only, no browser, no print, 08:45 resend, 09:15 release."""
184	    if not token_gate.token_needed():
185	        if not auto:                            # at 06:00 token_ping has already said this; stay quiet
186	            notify("[E1] Kite token already fresh on the laptop — no login needed.")
187	        return
188	    srv, err = _run_port80(port)
189	    if srv is None:
190	        # port 80 not bindable here — fall back to the LAN paste gate
191	        notify(f"\U0001f64b [E1] Could not bind port 80 for auto-login ({type(err).__name__}); using "
192	               f"the paste gate instead. A login link + paste steps follow.")
193	        kite_login.main()          # the LAN paste-gate path (sends its own 🙋 + finishes on paste)
194	        return
195	    try:
196	        login = _login_url()
197	    except Exception as e:
198	        notify(f"\U0001f64b [E1] Kite keys missing ({type(e).__name__}) — cannot build the login link. "
199	               f"Do: set KITE_API_KEY/KITE_API_SECRET in USER env, then re-run START_E1_KITE.bat.")
200	        return
201	    if auto:
202	        try:
203	            outcome, _n = _auto_wait(login, _done)
204	        except ValueError:
205	            notify("\U0001f64b [E1] The Kite login link came out with a placeholder/empty api_key — NOT "
206	                   "sent. Do: set a real KITE_API_KEY at USER scope, then double-click START_E1_KITE.bat.")
207	            outcome = "timeout"
208	    else:
209	        announce_login(login)      # R-KITE-LOGIN-FIX (1): open in the browser AND print it; 🙋 fallback
210	        notify("\U0001f64b [E1] Kite login — one tap. Do: open this link in the LAPTOP browser and log in; "
211	               "it completes by itself (no copy/paste): " + login)
212	        # wait for completion or the hard deadline, then release port 80
213	        _done.wait(HARD_MINUTES * 60)
214	        outcome = "landed" if _done.is_set() else "timeout"
215	    try:
216	        srv.shutdown(); srv.server_close()
217	    except Exception:
218	        pass
219	    if outcome != "landed":
220	        notify("\U0001f64b [E1] The laptop login window timed out and port 80 was released. "
221	               "Do: double-click START_E1_KITE.bat and log in on the LAPTOP when ready.")

2	"""R-AUTO-LOGIN-LINK — the dry run, runnable any night the market is closed.
3	
4	Proves the 06:00 path end to end WITHOUT a real login: a temp environment supplies a FAKE api key,
5	a fake clock says 06:00 IST, the catcher binds an EPHEMERAL port instead of 80, and the redirect it
6	catches carries a FAKE request token that a stub exchanges. Nothing reaches Telegram (every send is
7	captured by a sink, and notify's own demo/test guard would drop it anyway), nothing reaches Kite,
8	and no real token file is read or written.
9	
10	What it convicts:
11	  1. the exact 🙋 line, and that the api key cannot reach notify's 60-character send-log excerpt
12	  2. token_ping's 06:00 branch launches the catcher --auto, hidden and detached, never twice
13	  3. the catcher sends ONE 🙋 carrying the URL built from the STORED key, then CATCHES the redirect
14	  4. the 08:45 resend fires exactly once, and never a third time
15	
16	Run:  python tests/test_auto_login_link.py
17	"""

20	import threading
21	import time

29	IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
30	FAKE_KEY = "drykey000000dryz"          # a fake, never a real key; not DEMOKEY, so it is "sendable"
31	FAKE_REQ_TOKEN = "DRYRUN-REQUEST-TOKEN-NOT-REAL"

34	class _Sink(list):
35	    def __call__(self, msg):
36	        self.append(msg)
37	        return True

40	def _temp_env():
41	    """A temp environment with FAKE Kite keys. Process env wins over USER scope at the read seam."""
42	    old = {k: os.environ.get(k) for k in ("KITE_API_KEY", "KITE_API_SECRET")}
43	    os.environ["KITE_API_KEY"] = FAKE_KEY
44	    os.environ["KITE_API_SECRET"] = "dryrun-not-a-real-secret"
45	    return old
46	
47	
48	def _restore(old):
49	    for k, v in old.items():
50	        if v is None:
51	            os.environ.pop(k, None)
52	        else:
53	            os.environ[k] = v

58	def test_summons_text_and_no_key_in_the_send_log_excerpt():
59	    url = "https://kite.trade/connect/login?api_key=%s&v=3" % FAKE_KEY
60	    line = kite_catcher.summons(url)
61	    assert line.startswith("\U0001f64b Tap on the laptop to log in"), line
62	    assert line.endswith(url)
63	    # notify._log_send writes only the first 60 characters. The key must start beyond them, or the
64	    # secret lands in a committed .tsv.
65	    assert line.index(FAKE_KEY) > 60, "the api key would reach notify's 60-char send-log excerpt"
66	    assert FAKE_KEY not in line[:60]
67	    # a placeholder/empty URL is refused rather than sent
68	    for bad in ("https://kite.trade/connect/login?api_key=[redacted]&v=3",
69	                "https://kite.trade/connect/login?api_key=", ""):
70	        try:
71	            kite_catcher.summons(bad)
72	            raise AssertionError("summons wrapped an invalid URL: %r" % bad)
73	        except ValueError:
74	            pass

87	    assert args[0].lower().endswith("pythonw.exe") or os.name != "nt"
88	    assert kw["creationflags"] & L.NO_WINDOW and kw["creationflags"] & L.DETACHED
89	    assert kw["cwd"] == token_ping.REPO
90	
91	    # already up -> nothing started (a second catcher would lose the port and fall back to paste)
92	    running = [r"pythonw  scripts\kite_catcher.py --auto"]
93	    assert token_ping.start_catcher(_popen=popen, _running=running) is False
94	    assert len(started) == 1

97	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3. send + catch, end to end
98	
99	def test_0600_path_sends_the_link_and_catches_the_redirect():
100	    old_env = _temp_env()
101	    saved = {n: getattr(kite_catcher, n) for n in ("notify", "_ist", "kite", "_finish", "token_gate")}
102	    sink = _Sink()
103	    caught = []
104	
105	    class _StubKite:
106	        login_url = staticmethod(saved["kite"].login_url)   # the REAL builder, on the fake temp key
107	
108	        @staticmethod
109	        def exchange_token(rt):
110	            caught.append(rt)
111	            return {"provenance": "dry-run"}
112	
113	    class _StubGate:
114	        @staticmethod
115	        def token_needed(*a, **k):
116	            return True
117	
118	    six_am = datetime(2026, 9, 21, 6, 0, tzinfo=IST)
119	    kite_catcher.notify = sink
120	    kite_catcher._ist = lambda: six_am              # 06:00 IST: before both 08:45 and the 09:15 release
121	    kite_catcher.kite = _StubKite
122	    kite_catcher.token_gate = _StubGate
123	    kite_catcher._finish = kite_catcher._done.set   # skip post_login (it starts recorders for real)
124	    kite_catcher._done.clear()
125	
126	    try:
127	        import socket
128	        s = socket.socket()
129	        s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
130	        port = s.getsockname()[1]
131	        s.close()
132	
133	        t = threading.Thread(target=kite_catcher.main, kwargs={"auto": True, "port": port}, daemon=True)
134	        t.start()
135	
136	        for _ in range(200):                        # wait for the 🙋 (the port is up by then)
137	            if sink:
138	                break
139	            time.sleep(0.05)
140	        assert sink, "the 06:00 path sent nothing"
141	        assert len(sink) == 1, sink
142	        line = sink[0]
143	        assert line.startswith("\U0001f64b Tap on the laptop to log in"), line
144	        assert FAKE_KEY in line, "the link was not built from the stored key"
145	        assert "DEMOKEY" not in line
146	
147	        login = line[len(kite_catcher.SUMMONS):]
148	        assert kite_catcher.valid_login_url(login), login
149	
150	        # the redirect Kite would send back, with a FAKE request token
151	        url = "http://127.0.0.1:%d/?action=login&type=login&status=success&request_token=%s" % (
152	            port, FAKE_REQ_TOKEN)
153	        with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=10) as r:
154	            body = r.read().decode("utf-8")
155	        assert r.status == 200 and "login complete" in body.lower(), body
156	        assert caught == [FAKE_REQ_TOKEN], caught
157	
158	        t.join(timeout=15)
159	        assert not t.is_alive(), "the catcher did not release the port after the token landed"
160	        assert len(sink) == 1, "a second 🙋 was sent after the token landed: %r" % (sink,)
161	    finally:
162	        for n, v in saved.items():
163	            setattr(kite_catcher, n, v)
164	        kite_catcher._done.clear()
165	        _restore(old_env)

168	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 4. the 08:45 resend, once
169	
170	def test_resend_at_0845_fires_exactly_once():
171	    url = "https://kite.trade/connect/login?api_key=%s&v=3" % FAKE_KEY
172	    # 06:00 in 30-min steps out past the 15:30 release (A7): reaches 08:45 (the ONE resend) and then
173	    # crosses RELEASE_IST so the wait ends in a timeout. The last tick sits after 15:30 so the loop always
174	    # terminates even if the clock is read more than once per iteration.
175	    ticks = [datetime(2026, 9, 21, 6, 0, tzinfo=IST) + timedelta(minutes=30 * i) for i in range(24)]
176	    it = iter(ticks)

FAULT 1 — the camera being down at 08:15/09:09                              DONE
  What happened: the order-book depth recorder (the camera the 08:15 READY line
  needs) was started at 06:02 when the laptop had only 0.69 GB of memory free —
  below the 1.2 GB floor a recorder needs — so it didn't survive. The watchdog then
  refused to restart it (correctly, RAM was too low) but said NOTHING, so the camera
  stayed down until 09:09 with no one told.
  Fix: the watchdog still won't force a recorder into too-little memory (that would
  just crash-loop), but it now posts ONE plain ⏱ line naming the measured free RAM
  and the floor, and keeps retrying every cycle — it starts the camera the moment
  memory frees. Never silent again.
  Test went red then green (test_watchdog_recorders 5/5).

FAULT 2 — the builder working during market hours                          DONE
  What happened: a build session was running 08:02–08:29, after the 08:30 pause,
  because the auto-continue had no market-hours lock.
  Fix: auto-continue now refuses to reopen a session inside the 08:30–15:45 pause
  (using the desk's one clock; if that clock ever errors it treats the time as
  "paused", so a fault can never reopen a session in market hours by accident).
  Test went red (it failed with no such lock) then green (test_session_resurrect_
  markethours 3/3).

FAULT 3 — the false "tap to log in" at 08:45                               DONE
  What happened: the login link was resent at 08:45 even though the token had been
  present since 06:03. Cause: the resend only checked whether THIS process had
  caught the login, not whether a token existed at all — so a token that arrived any
  other way still triggered the resend.
  Fix: before resending, it now re-checks whether a token is genuinely present; if
  one is, it stops and sends nothing. A login 🙋 fires only when the token is truly
  absent.
  Test went red (the token re-check didn't exist) then green (test_auto_login_link
